```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function optimizer.

Minimizes:
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)
for nonnegative sequences.  Scale is invariant, so all internal optimization
is done on the probability simplex (sum = 1).

Main ideas in this version:
- Multiple diverse starts: incumbent, endpoint power laws, asymmetric powers,
  trigonometric/U-shaped profiles, and random distributions.
- Full weighted-gradient mirror descent.  Instead of only a few hand-picked
  top lags, gradients are computed for Lp-like probability weights and for
  randomized top-active lag weights.  The weighted gradient is computed by
  an FFT diagonal convolution, so it is cheap even for n ~ 6000-16000.
- Simulated-annealing acceptance, adaptive step size, and resets to the
  incumbent to avoid long excursions into bad basins.
- A cross-convolution alternating phase approximating the LP direction from
  the state-of-the-art approach: for fixed f, optimize g to make f*g flat,
  then blend g into f.
- A coordinate/pair polish phase for final local improvements.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _fft_size(total):
    """Smallest power of two >= total."""
    if total <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (total - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(x, floor=0.0):
    """Clip to [0,1000], remove non-finite values, normalize sum to 1."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        x = np.maximum(x, float(floor))
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return x / s


def _convolution(f):
    """Linear convolution f*f, clipped to nonnegative, length 2n-1."""
    n = f.size
    total = 2 * n - 1
    size = _fft_size(total)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)
    c[:total] = np.maximum(c[:total], 0.0)
    return c[:total]


def _cross_convolution(a, b):
    """Linear convolution a*b, clipped to nonnegative, length 2n-1."""
    n = a.size
    total = 2 * n - 1
    size = _fft_size(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, size)
    c[:total] = np.maximum(c[:total], 0.0)
    return c[:total]


def _weighted_density_gradient(fixed, weights):
    """
    For L(w) = sum_k w_k * (fixed*f)_k, returns 2 * dL/df when f=fixed.

    If used for cross-convolution with a fixed first factor, multiply by 0.5
    to get the derivative with respect to the second factor.
    """
    n = fixed.size
    L = weights.size
    total = L + n - 1
    size = _fft_size(total)

    Fr = np.fft.rfft(fixed[::-1], size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * Fr, size)

    # grad_i = 2 * sum_j weights[i+j] * fixed[j]
    grad = 2.0 * h[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    np.maximum(grad, 0.0, out=grad)
    return grad


def _make_weights_lpm(c, beta):
    """
    Probability weights proportional to c^beta.

    beta -> 0 gives uniform weights, large beta focuses on large coefficients.
    """
    L = c.size
    P = float(c.max())
    if L == 0 or P <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(P):
        return np.ones(max(L, 1), dtype=np.float64) / max(L, 1)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    floor = P * 1e-10
    cp = np.maximum(c, floor)
    logw = beta * (np.log(cp) - np.log(P))
    logw = np.clip(logw, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(logw)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _make_weights_top(c, K, rng=None):
    """
    Probabilities supported on the top K coefficients.
    If rng is given, use a Dirichlet-mixed random distribution over those K.
    """
    L = c.size
    K = int(max(1, min(int(K), L)))
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        part = np.argpartition(c, L - K)
        idx = part[L - K:]

    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 1:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        if rng is None:
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        else:
            alpha = max(0.5, min(20.0, 0.2 * float(K)))
            try:
                wv = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) * float(alpha))
            except Exception:
                wv = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            # Mix a uniform component to avoid too-sparse random active sets.
            wv = 0.7 * wv + 0.3 * (1.0 / float(K))
            w[idx] = wv

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _mirror_update(f, grad, eta, floor=1e-18):
    """
    Exponentiated-gradient / mirror step on the simplex.

    f_new_i = f_i * exp(-eta * normalized_grad_i), then normalize.
    """
    if eta <= 0.0:
        return f.copy()

    gmax = float(grad.max())
    gmin = float(grad.min())
    scale = gmax - gmin

    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-300:
        fn = f + float(floor)
        s = float(fn.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
            return fn / s
        return f.copy()

    ratio = (grad - gmin) / scale
    ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
    f_new = f * np.exp(-float(eta) * ratio)
    f_new = np.maximum(f_new, 0.0)
    f_new = f_new + float(floor)

    s = float(f_new.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return f.copy()
    return f_new / s


def _downsample(x, target):
    """Average/interpolate down to target length."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    m = x.size
    target = int(target)
    if m <= target:
        return x
    k = max(1, int(m // target))
    out = []
    i = 0
    while i < m:
        j = min(i + k, m)
        out.append(float(np.mean(x[i:j])))
        i = j
    arr = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == target:
        return arr
    old_pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    new_pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target)
    return np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, arr)


def _generate_starts(base, n, rng, count=100):
    """Generate diverse nonnegative starting shapes."""
    base = _normalize(base)
    raw = []

    # Incumbent variants.
    raw.append(base.copy())
    raw.append(base[::-1].copy())

    # Sharpen / smooth incumbent.
    for p in [0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]:
        b = np.power(base + 1e-30, float(p))
        raw.append(_normalize(b))

    # Asymmetric re-weightings of incumbent.
    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    for eps in [0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8]:
        b = base * (1.0 + float(eps) * (x - 0.5))
        raw.append(_normalize(b))
        b = base * (1.0 + float(eps) * (2.0 * np.abs(x - 0.5)))
        raw.append(_normalize(b))

    # Endpoint power laws.  Single-sided x^-0.5 is a strong analytic prior.
    i1 = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    rn = np.arange(n, 0, -1, dtype=np.float64)

    alphas = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.45, 0.48, 0.49, 0.50, 0.52, 0.55]
    for a in alphas:
        raw.append(_normalize(i1 ** (-float(a))))
        raw.append(_normalize(rn ** (-float(a))))

    # Weak two-sided / asymmetric endpoint powers.
    gammas = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.45, 0.49]
    for a in [0.40, 0.45, 0.49, 0.50]:
        for g in gammas:
            for D in [0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0]:
                b = i1 ** (-float(a)) + float(D) * (rn ** (-float(g)))
                raw.append(_normalize(b))
                b = rn ** (-float(a)) + float(D) * (i1 ** (-float(g)))
                raw.append(_normalize(b))

    # U-shaped and linear profiles.
    for alpha in [1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0]:
        raw.append(_normalize(1.0 + float(alpha) * x * x))
        raw.append(_normalize(1.0 + float(alpha) * (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x)))
        raw.append(_normalize(1.0 + float(alpha) * np.abs(x - 0.5)))

    # Low-frequency wobbles.
    for m in [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7]:
        for amp in [0.3, 0.7, 1.2]:
            raw.append(_normalize(1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x)))
            raw.append(_normalize(1.0 + float(amp) * np.abs(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x))))

    if len(raw) > count:
        raw = raw[:count]

    # Random fill.
    while len(raw) < count:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.15:
            raw.append(_normalize(rng.exponential(1.0, n)))
        elif r < 0.30:
            alpha = 0.05 + 0.20 * float(rng.random())
            raw.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * alpha))
        elif r < 0.50:
            raw.append(_normalize(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.01))
        elif r < 0.70:
            scale = 0.05 + 0.20 * float(rng.random())
            raw.append(_normalize(base + (scale / math.sqrt(float(n))) * rng.standard_normal(n)))
        elif r < 0.85:
            k = max(1, n // 5)
            b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=k)
            b[idx] = rng.exponential(1.0, k)
            raw.append(_normalize(b))
        else:
            a = float(rng.random()) * 0.7
            g = float(rng.random()) * 0.4
            D = 0.1 + 0.9 * float(rng.random())
            b = i1 ** (-a) + D * (rn ** (-g))
            raw.append(_normalize(b))

    return raw[:count]


def _local_mirror(f0, deadline, rng, n, initial_eta=0.45):
    """
    Main local optimizer: stochastic weighted-gradient mirror descent.
    Uses randomized top-lag weights and Lp-like full weights alternately.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    c = _convolution(f)
    P = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(P):
        f = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        c = _convolution(f)
        P = float(c.max())

    best = P
    best_f = f.copy()

    eta = float(initial_eta)
    P0 = max(P, 1e-30)
    T = max(1e-15, P0 * 0.002)
    T_min = P0 * 1e-8
    T_max = P0 * 0.05

    rejects = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = start
    run_budget = max(1.0, deadline - start)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if not np.isfinite(P):
            f = best_f.copy()
            c = _convolution(f)
            P = float(c.max())

        if P < best - 1e-15:
            best = P
            best_f = f.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            rejects = 0
            T = max(T_min, T * 0.98)

        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed_frac = min(1.0, (now - start) / run_budget)
        stuck_frac = min(1.0, (now - last_improve) / 45.0)

        # Schedule: early = moderate top sets; middle = stronger Lp / larger
        # top sets; late = focus on peaks but allow occasional broad escape.
        if elapsed_frac < 0.15:
            use_top = rng.random() < 0.75
            K_list = [2, 3, 5, 8]
            beta_list = [4.0, 16.0, 64.0]
        elif elapsed_frac < 0.55:
            use_top = rng.random() < 0.45
            K_list = [4, 8, 16, 32]
            beta_list = [16.0, 64.0, 256.0, 1024.0]
        else:
            use_top = rng.random() < 0.35
            K_list = [2, 5, 10, 20]
            beta_list = [64.0, 256.0, 1024.0, 4096.0]

        if use_top:
            K = K_list[int(rng.integers(0, len(K_list)))]
            if stuck_frac > 0.5:
                K = int(min(128, K * (1 + int(stuck_frac * 3))))
            w = _make_weights_top(c, K, rng)
        else:
            beta = beta_list[int(rng.integers(0, len(beta_list)))]
            if stuck_frac > 0.7:
                beta *= 2.0
            w = _make_weights_lpm(c, beta)

        grad = _weighted_density_gradient(f, w)
        gscale = float(grad.max())

        if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale < 1e-300:
            f = f + (0.005 / math.sqrt(float(n))) * rng.standard_normal(n)
            f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
            s = float(f.sum())
            f = f / s if s > 1e-300 else _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
            c = _convolution(f)
            P = float(c.max())
            it += 1
            continue

        if rejects > 4:
            mults = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
        else:
            mults = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0)

        best_trial_P = np.inf
        best_trial_f = None
        best_trial_c = None
        best_mult = 1.0

        for mult in mults:
            f_try = _mirror_update(f, grad, float(eta) * float(mult))
            c_try = _convolution(f_try)
            P_try = float(c_try.max())
            if P_try < best_trial_P:
                best_trial_P = P_try
                best_trial_f = f_try
                best_trial_c = c_try
                best_mult = float(mult)

        if best_trial_f is None:
            it += 1
            continue

        delta = best_trial_P - P
        accept = False
        if delta <= 1e-15:
            accept = True
        elif delta < T and rejects > 1:
            ratio = delta / T
            if ratio < 50.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(-ratio):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            f = best_trial_f
            c = best_trial_c
            P = best_trial_P
            eta *= best_mult
            if delta <= 0.0:
                eta = min(5.0, eta * 1.05)
            else:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.90)
                T = min(T_max, T * 1.10)
        else:
            rejects += 1
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.70)
            if rejects > 8:
                # Return near incumbent with modest noise.
                f = best_f.copy()
                f = f + (0.010 / math.sqrt(float(n))) * rng.standard_normal(n)
                f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
                s = float(f.sum())
                f = f / s if s > 1e-300 else _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
                c = _convolution(f)
                P = float(c.max())
                rejects = 0
                T = min(T_max, T * 1.50)

        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"mirror it {it}: P={P:.10f} score={2.0 * n * P:.6f} "
                  f"best={best:.10f} score={2.0 * n * best:.6f} eta={eta:.3f}")

    if P < best:
        best = P
        best_f = f.copy()
    return best_f.copy(), 2.0 * float(n) * best


def _alternating_cross(f0, deadline, rng, n):
    """
    Alternating optimization inspired by the LP-direction method.

    For fixed f, optimize g to make the cross-convolution f*g flatter, then
    blend g into f.  This approximates the previous LP-based lifting step.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    c = _convolution(f)
    P = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(P):
        f = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        c = _convolution(f)
        P = float(c.max())

    best = P
    best_f = f.copy()

    g = f.copy()
    eta = 0.40
    rejects = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = start
    run_budget = max(1.0, deadline - start)
    outer = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if P < best - 1e-15:
            best = P
            best_f = f.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            rejects = 0

        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed_frac = min(1.0, (now - start) / run_budget)
        stuck_frac = min(1.0, (now - last_improve) / 30.0)

        # Inner cross-optimization for a short time slice.
        inner_deadline = min(deadline - 0.05, time.monotonic() + 0.25)
        while time.monotonic() < inner_deadline:
            x = _cross_convolution(f, g)
            max_x = float(x.max())
            if not np.isfinite(max_x):
                break

            use_lp = rng.random() < 0.6
            if use_lp:
                beta = 8.0 + 128.0 * elapsed_frac + 64.0 * stuck_frac
                w = _make_weights_lpm(x, beta)
            else:
                K = 2 + int(16.0 * elapsed_frac)
                if stuck_frac > 0.6:
                    K = min(64, K + 16)
                w = _make_weights_top(x, K, rng)

            grad = _weighted_density_gradient(f, w) * 0.5
            gscale = float(grad.max())
            if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale < 1e-300:
                break

            best_g = None
            best_mx = np.inf
            best_mult = 1.0
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5):
                g_try = _mirror_update(g, grad, float(eta) * float(mult))
                x_try = _cross_convolution(f, g_try)
                mx = float(x_try.max())
                if mx < best_mx:
                    best_mx = mx
                    best_g = g_try
                    best_mult = float(mult)

            if best_g is not None and best_mx <= max_x + 1e-15:
                g = best_g
                eta = min(2.0, eta * best_mult * 1.05)
                rejects = 0
            else:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.75)
                rejects += 1
                if rejects > 6:
                    g = f.copy()
                    rejects = 0

        g = _normalize(g)
        cg = _convolution(g)
        Pg = float(cg.max())

        improved = False
        for mix in (0.30, 0.60, 0.90):
            fm = (1.0 - mix) * f + mix * g
            fm = np.maximum(fm, 0.0)
            fm = _normalize(fm)
            cm = _convolution(fm)
            Pm = float(cm.max())
            if Pm < P - 1e-15:
                f = fm
                c = cm
                P = Pm
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved and Pg < P - 1e-15:
            f = g
            c = cg
            P = Pg
            improved = True

        if improved:
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
        else:
            if time.monotonic() - last_improve > 30.0:
                g = f.copy()
                f = f + (0.01 / math.sqrt(float(n))) * rng.standard_normal(n)
                f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
                s = float(f.sum())
                f = f / s if s > 1e-300 else _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
                c = _convolution(f)
                P = float(c.max())

        outer += 1
        if outer % 200 == 0:
            print(f"alternating outer {outer}: P={P:.10f} score={2.0 * n * P:.6f} "
                  f"best={best:.10f} score={2.0 * n * best:.6f}")

    if P < best:
        best = P
        best_f = f.copy()
    return best_f.copy(), 2.0 * float(n) * best


def _polish(f0, deadline, rng, n):
    """Coordinate / pair-mass polish using full FFT evaluations."""
    f = _normalize(f0)
    c = _convolution(f)
    P = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(P):
        f = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        c = _convolution(f)
        P = float(c.max())

    best = P
    best_f = f.copy()
    passes = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if P < best - 1e-15:
            best = P
            best_f = f.copy()
            passes = 0

        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n, k + 1)
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi)
        contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        contrib[idx] = 2.0 * f[k - idx]

        improved = False

        # Structured moves: push mass from high derivative coordinates to low ones.
        if n > 20:
            d_order = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:25]
            r_order = np.argsort(contrib)[:35]
        else:
            d_order = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
            r_order = np.argsort(contrib)

        for d in d_order[:14]:
            if f[d] < 1e-15:
                continue
            for r in r_order[:14]:
                if r == d:
                    continue
                for frac in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40):
                    amt = f[d] * float(frac)
                    if amt <= 1e-18:
                        continue
                    ft = f.copy()
                    ft[d] -= amt
                    ft[r] += amt
                    ft = np.maximum(ft, 0.0)
                    s = float(ft.sum())
                    if s <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    ft /= s
                    ct = _convolution(ft)
                    pt = float(ct.max())
                    if pt < P - 1e-15:
                        f = ft
                        c = ct
                        P = pt
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            continue

        # Random pair moves.
        for _ in range(6):
            d = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            r = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if d == r:
                continue
            if f[d] < f[r]:
                d, r = r, d
            frac = (0.01, 0.03, 0.10)[int(rng.integers(0, 3))]
            amt = f[d] * float(frac)
            if amt <= 1e-18:
                continue
            ft = f.copy()
            ft[d] -= amt
            ft[r] += amt
            ft = np.maximum(ft, 0.0)
            s = float(ft.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            ft /= s
            ct = _convolution(ft)
            pt = float(ct.max())
            if pt < P - 1e-15:
                f = ft
                c = ct
                P = pt
                improved = True
                break

        # Occasional small global perturbation.
        if not improved and passes % 25 == 0:
            ft = f + (0.002 / math.sqrt(float(n))) * rng.standard_normal(n)
            ft = np.maximum(ft, 0.0)
            ft = _normalize(ft)
            ct = _convolution(ft)
            pt = float(ct.max())
            if pt < P - 1e-15:
                f = ft
                c = ct
                P = pt

        passes += 1

        if passes % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"polish pass {passes}: P={P:.10f} score={2.0 * n * P:.6f} "
                  f"best={best:.10f} score={2.0 * n * best:.6f}")

    if P < best:
        best = P
        best_f = f.copy()
    return best_f.copy(), 2.0 * float(n) * best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best nonnegative coefficient sequence within the time budget.
    Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    overall_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total_budget - 30.0)

    # Load starting construction if available.
    try:
        raw_base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        raw_base = None

    if raw_base is None or raw_base.size < 512:
        n = 6121
        i1 = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        raw_base = i1 ** (-0.5)
    elif raw_base.size > 16384:
        raw_base = _downsample(raw_base, 16384)

    raw_base = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(raw_base, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(raw_base.size)
    base = _normalize(raw_base)

    print(f"search length n={n}")

    # Generate and score starts.
    start_count = 120 if n <= 8192 else 60
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    starts = _generate_starts(base, n, rng, count=start_count)
    scored = []
    counter = 0
    for s in starts:
        f = _normalize(s)
        c = _convolution(f)
        p = float(c.max())
        if np.isfinite(p):
            scored.append((p, counter, f.copy()))
            counter += 1

    # Ensure the incumbent / base is present.
    cb = _convolution(base)
    pb = float(cb.max())
    if np.isfinite(pb):
        scored.append((pb, counter, base.copy()))
        counter += 1

    if not scored:
        base = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        cb = _convolution(base)
        scored = [(float(cb.max()), 0, base.copy())]

    scored.sort()
    best_f = scored[0][2].copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * scored[0][0]
    print(f"starts={len(scored)} in {time.monotonic() - t0:.2f}s, "
          f"best initial score={best_score:.6f}")

    if len(scored) > 5:
        print("top initial scores: " +
              ", ".join(f"{2.0 * n * p:.6f}" for p, _, _ in scored[:5]))

    # Phase 1: short runs from several good starts.
    remaining = overall_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 80.0:
        initial_total = min(remaining * 0.22, max(60.0, 0.20 * total_budget), 650.0)
        if n > 10000:
            initial_total *= 0.55
        num_initial = min(len(scored), 4 if n > 10000 else 6)
        if num_initial > 0 and initial_total > 40.0:
            slice_i = initial_total / float(num_initial)
            for i in range(num_initial):
                now = time.monotonic()
                rem = overall_deadline - now
                if rem < 40.0:
                    break
                alloc = min(slice_i, rem - 30.0)
                if alloc < 15.0:
                    break
                peak_i, _, f_i = scored[i]
                print(f"\ninitial run {i + 1}/{num_initial}, start score={2.0 * n * peak_i:.6f}, budget={alloc:.0f}s")
                f_loc, score_loc = _local_mirror(f_i, now + alloc, rng, n, initial_eta=0.40)
                if score_loc < best_score:
                    best_score = score_loc
                    best_f = f_loc.copy()
                    print(f"  improved to {best_score:.6f}")

    # Phase 2: main local optimization from current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = overall_deadline - now
    if rem > 80.0:
        main_budget = min(rem * 0.45, max(240.0, 0.35 * total_budget), 1500.0)
        if n > 10000:
            main_budget *= 0.7
        alloc = min(main_budget, rem - 40.0)
        if alloc > 30.0:
            print(f"\nmain mirror run budget={alloc:.0f}s")
            f_loc, score_loc = _local_mirror(best_f, now + alloc, rng, n, initial_eta=0.45)
            if score_loc < best_score:
                best_score = score_loc
                best_f = f_loc.copy()
                print(f"  improved to {best_score:.6f}")

    # Phase 3: alternating cross-convolution / LP-direction phase.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = overall_deadline - now
    if rem > 80.0:
        alt_budget = min(rem * 0.28, max(120.0, 0.16 * total_budget), 700.0)
        if n > 10000:
            alt_budget *= 0.6
        alloc = min(alt_budget, rem - 50.0)
        if alloc > 30.0:
            print(f"\nalternating cross phase budget={alloc:.0f}s")
            f_alt, score_alt = _alternating_cross(best_f, now + alloc, rng, n)
            if score_alt < best_score:
                best_score = score_alt
                best_f = f_alt.copy()
                print(f"  improved to {best_score:.6f}")

    # After alternating, do another local polish from the current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = overall_deadline - now
    if rem > 70.0:
        post_budget = min(rem * 0.25, max(90.0, 0.12 * total_budget), 450.0)
        if n > 10000:
            post_budget *= 0.6
        alloc = min(post_budget, rem - 40.0)
        if alloc > 30.0:
            print(f"\npost-alternating mirror budget={alloc:.0f}s")
            f_loc, score_loc = _local_mirror(best_f, now + alloc, rng, n, initial_eta=0.35)
            if score_loc < best_score:
                best_score = score_loc
                best_f = f_loc.copy()
                print(f"  improved to {best_score:.6f}")

    # Phase 4: coordinate polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = overall_deadline - now
    if rem > 60.0:
        pol_budget = min(rem * 0.30, max(90.0, 0.12 * total_budget), 450.0)
        if n > 10000:
            pol_budget *= 0.55
        alloc = min(pol_budget, rem - 35.0)
        if alloc > 20.0:
            print(f"\npolish budget={alloc:.0f}s")
            f_pol, score_pol = _polish(best_f, now + alloc, rng, n)
            if score_pol < best_score:
                best_score = score_pol
                best_f = f_pol.copy()
                print(f"  improved to {best_score:.6f}")

    # Phase 5: final local run if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = overall_deadline - now
    if rem > 45.0:
        final_local_budget = min(rem * 0.80, 260.0)
        alloc = min(final_local_budget, rem - 25.0)
        if alloc > 15.0:
            print(f"\nfinal local budget={alloc:.0f}s")
            f_loc, score_loc = _local_mirror(best_f, now + alloc, rng, n, initial_eta=0.25)
            if score_loc < best_score:
                best_score = score_loc
                best_f = f_loc.copy()
                print(f"  improved to {best_score:.6f}")

    # Final official evaluations on a few safe candidates.
    candidate_list = [best_f, base]
    if len(scored) > 1 and n <= 10000 and (overall_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 35.0:
        candidate_list.append(scored[0][2])
    if len(scored) > 2 and n <= 8000 and (overall_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 50.0:
        candidate_list.append(scored[1][2])

    final_seq = best_f
    final_val = np.inf

    for cand in candidate_list:
        if overall_deadline - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
            break
        fc = np.clip(np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
        fc = _normalize(fc)
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fc])
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < final_val:
            final_val = val
            final_seq = fc

    # Fallback if official evaluator failed.
    if not np.isfinite(final_val):
        fb = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) ** (-0.5)
        fb = _normalize(fb)
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fb])
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(val):
            final_val = val
            final_seq = fb
        else:
            final_seq = best_f
            final_val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(_convolution(best_f).max())

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_seq]
    print(f"internal best FFT score={best_score:.6f}")
    print(f"official best score={final_val:.6f}")
    return final_list
```
